Runner arguments (from source):

- `--notes; default=[1000, 10000]`
- `--body-chars; default=4096`
- `--history; default=100000`
- `--repeats; default=5`
- `--page-size; choices=[4096, 16384]; default=4096`
- `--skip-build; action='store_true'; help='Use already refreshed app objects'`
- `--sqlcipher-build; help='Isolated source build containing sqlite3.h and libsqlite3.a'`
- `--zstd-prefix; help='Optional experiment-only prefix containing include/zstd.h and lib/libzstd.a'`
- `--zstd-level; choices=[1, 3, 6, 9, 15, 19]; default=3`
- `--zstd-min-match; choices=[0, 4, 6]; default=0; help="0 uses the selected level's default"`
- `--zstd-strategy; choices=['default', 'dfast']; default='default'`
- `--dictionary-size; choices=[0, 8192, 32768, 65536]; default=0; help='Train a private shared dictionary when recompressing current cells'`
- `--dictionary-pattern; choices=['words', 'mixed', 'random']; default='words'; help='Independent synthetic training family; may differ from measured notes'`
- `--dictionary-training; choices=['held-out', 'current', 'all-current']; default='held-out'; help='Independent generated records, a bounded current-cell sample, or every current cell (128-MiB fixture limit)'`
- `--dictionary-sample-limit; default=1024; help='Maximum current cells considered for sampled training'`
- `--dictionary-sample-budget; default=8 * 1024 * 1024; help='Maximum total decoded bytes retained for sampled training'`
- `--dictionary-contracts; action='store_true'; help='Small-fixture dictionary faults and abrupt-exit transaction checks'`
- `--expect-training-fallback; action='store_true'; help='Negative control: require training to decline and complete startup without dictionary cells'`
- `--dictionary-micro-baseline; help='Additional alternating decode-only comparison with a matching no-dictionary store.sqlite'`
- `--dictionary-example-comparison; action='store_true'; help='Compare strict wrapper, official-example core API, and refDDict API on validated frames'`
- `--key-mode; choices=['none', 'raw', 'pass']; default='none'`
- `--aged; action='store_true'; help='Actual-DDL, clean-exit locality experiment; ignores --kinds'`
- `--churn-rounds; default=10`
- `--context-bytes; default=256; help='Synthetic authoring-reference bytes per current cell'`
- `--layouts; choices=LAYOUTS; default=['aged', 'rebuilt', 'compacted']`
- `--secondary; action='store_true'; help='Also vary page-cache size, temporary lifetimes and raw reads'`
- `--reuse-aged-root; help='Reuse completed clean fixture images; write new measurements separately'`
- `--source-layout; choices=LAYOUTS; help='Use this layout from --reuse-aged-root as the immutable source for NEW variants'`
- `--rewrite-passes; default=1; help='Repeat the chosen physical layout operation without intervening logical changes'`
- `--rewrite-cache-kib; default=0; help='0 preserves the existing maintenance connection default'`
- `--rewrite-spill; choices=['default', 'off']; default='default'`
- `--rewrite-secure-delete; choices=['default', 'off']; default='default'; help='Synthetic cost-isolation control only; not a privacy policy change'`
- `--rewrite-chunk-mib; default=0; help='Native SQLite VFS chunk-size hint; not a B-tree reservation'`
- `--reserve-budget-mib; default=16; help='Maximum temporary payload in bounded-reserved control'`
- `--read-ahead; choices=['default', 'off']; default='default'; help="Aged probe's measured read descriptor only"`
- `--hole-interval; default=0; help='Retain then release a 16-KiB private buffer every N mutations (0=off)'`
- `--cell-codec; choices=['raw', 'zlib', 'zstd']; default='raw'; help='Aged experiment only: independently compressed current cells'`
- `--cell-decoder; choices=['allocate', 'scratch', 'stream']; default='allocate'; help='Per-cell allocation baseline, reusable output buffer, or buffer plus reusable inflater'`
- `--body-pattern; choices=['words', 'random', 'mixed']; default='words'; help='Aged experiment: repeated words, high-entropy ASCII, or six mixed note genres'`
- `--prefetch-workers; choices=[1, 4, 8, 16]; default=1; help='Bounded raw ciphertext read-ahead concurrency'`
- `--prefetch-amplification; choices=[1, 2, 4]; default=1; help='Coalesce ranges with at most this ratio of read pages to requested current pages'`
- `--hint-fault; choices=['none', 'empty', 'malformed', 'wrong-pages']; default='none'; help='Read-ahead hint fault injection; all note values must still come from ordinary SQL'`
- `--aged-kinds; choices=['table', 'uuid', 'flat', 'compressed', 'zstd', 'table-prefetch']; default=['table', 'uuid', 'flat', 'compressed']`
- `--conditions; choices=['warm', 'cold', 'nocache']; default=['warm', 'cold', 'nocache']`
- `--kinds; choices=['frozen-file', 'frozen', 'flat-file', 'flat', 'compressed', 'rows', 'rows-direct']; default=['frozen-file', 'frozen', 'flat-file', 'flat', 'compressed', 'rows', 'rows-direct']`
